The full refine details:
```
def refine(a, max_seconds, rng):
    a = np.asarray(a,float)
    if invalid: return a, score
    a = np.clip(a,0,None)
    s=a.sum(); if s<1e-8: uniform
    a=a/s
    a=np.clip(a,1e-6,None); a/=a.sum()
    z=np.log(a)
    best=a.copy(); best_score=evaluate...
    t=1
    beta1=0.9; beta2=0.999; m=np.zeros(n); v=zeros
    K = 64 maybe
    beta_p = n/50 initially; increase to n*10
    while time < deadline:
       a=softmax(z)
       a=np.clip(a,1e-12,None); a/=a.sum()
       c=fft_conv(a)
       # top idx
       if K < 2n-1: top = np.argpartition(c, -K)[-K:]
       else top = np.arange(2n-1)
       vals=c[top]
       # p weights exp((vals-vals.max())/temp_c)
       temp_c = 1.0 / beta_p
       p_full = zeros; p_full[top] = np.exp((vals-vals.max())/temp_c); p_full /= p_full.sum()
       # compute q = cross_corr(p_full,a)
       N=... (maybe precompute)
       q = ifft(conj(rfft(p_full,N))*rfft(a,N)).real
       q = q[np.mod(N - np.arange(1,n+1), N)]? q[j]=q[(N-j)%N]
       mgrad = dot(p_full, c)
       grad = 2*a*(q - mgrad)
       # random perturbation to break symmetry
       if rng.random()<0.01: z += 0.01*rng.normal(0,1,n)
       # Adam update
       ...
       z -= step
       # occasionally exact eval
       if t%20==0 or end: score=... update best
    return best, score
```

Implement coarse search:
```
def coarse_optimize(seed, budget_s, m=512, R=8):
    rng = default_rng(seed)
    x = (np.arange(m)+0.5)/m
    h = 2*x-1 (centered)
    basis = empty((R,m))
    basis[0]=h
    basis[1]=h*h - 1/12
    basis[2]=h**3
    basis[3]=np.sin(np.pi*x)
    basis[4]=np.cos(np.pi*x)
    basis[5]=np.sin(2πx)
    basis[6]=np.cos(2πx)
    basis[7]=np.sin(3πx)
    best_a=None; best_score=inf
    time limit.
    nstarts = e.g. 20
    for start in range(nstarts):
       p0 = rng.normal(0, scale, R); maybe biases.
       try:
          res = minimize(objective, p0, args=(basis,), method='Nelder-Mead', options={'maxiter':500,'xatol':1e-3,'fatol':1e-5})
       except: pass
       evaluate res.x; also p0
       update
    return best_a
```
Objective uses m? Need return score approximate. Since sum normalized, score = 2*m*max(c). But when interpolating to n, scale changes? We can use same formula with m. Target for continuous score 2 sup f*f. If m large enough, good. Use m=1024 for better; objective FFT 1024 fast. R=8, Nelder 500*20* maybe 10000 eval * 1024 log = maybe 1s? Good. m=1024 or 2048. Use m=min(2048, max(128,n//2))? Since n=4498, m=1024. Could use m=1024 or 2048. Objective for coarse:
```
def coarse_objective(params, basis, m):
    z = np.dot(basis, params)
    z -= z.max()
    a = np.exp(z)
    a = np.clip(a, 1e-6, None)
    s=a.sum()
    if s <= 1e-12: return 1e6
    a /= s
    c = fft_conv(a)
    val = 2*m*c.max()
    # small penalty for very nonuniform? maybe none
    return val
```
`np.dot(basis, params)` basis shape (R,m), params (R,) => (m,). Good.

Could include a parameter for entropy regularization? No.

After coarse, we get a length m. Interpolate to n:
```
def upscale_sequence(a, n, m):
    if m==n: return a.copy()
    x_old=(np.arange(m)+0.5)/m
    x_new=(np.arange(n)+0.5)/n
    b=np.interp(x_new, x_old, a)
    b=np.clip(b,0,None); s=b.sum(); return b/s
```
Linear interpolation of positive sequence. If coarse had abrupt changes, okay.

def softmax(z):
    z = z - z.max()

Implementation structure in propose:
```
def propose_candidate(...):
    rng=default_rng(seed)
    t0=time.monotonic(); total_deadline=t0+budget_s-5
    n_target = len(height_sequence_1) if available else 256
    candidates=[]
    # add parent normalized, uniform, maybe tilts
    # coarse search with time min(150, budget/4)
    coarse_best = coarse_search(...)
    candidates.append(upscale(coarse_best))
    # maybe short refine each candidate for 20s
    short_deadline=t0+60
    for cand in candidates[:4]:
       a,s=refine(cand, time_left_short,...)
       save
    # pick best, long refine with remaining-20
    a,s=refine(best, remaining,...)
    # final maybe another polish: add small perturbations and evaluate
    return best list
```

def fft_convolution(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,float)
    n=a.size
    if n==0: return np.array([])
    size=2*n-1
    N=1
    while N < size: N <<= 1
    fa=np.fft.rfft(a, N)
    out=np.fft.irfft(fa*fa, N)[:size]
    # clean
    out = np.where(out > 0, out, 0.0)? Convolution of nonnegative should nonnegative; tiny negative due FFT. But for max, negative irrelevant. Could set max only.
    return out
```

def coarse_candidate(seed, budget_s, m=1024):
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    deadline = time.monotonic()+max(0,budget_s)-2
    x=(np.arange(m)+0.5)/m
    h=2*x-1
    R=8
    B=np.empty((R,m))
    B[0]=h
    B[1]=h*h - 1.0/12.0
    B[2]=h*h*h
    B[3]=np.sin(np.pi*x)
    B[4]=np.cos(np.pi*x)
    B[5]=np.sin(2*np.pi*x)
    B[6]=np.cos(2*np.pi*x)
    B[7]=np.sin(3*np.pi*x)
    best_a=None; best_score=np.inf
    scale=1.2
    max_starts=12
    start=0
    for _ in range(max_starts):
       if time...
       p = rng.normal(0, scale, R)
       # bias some parameter
       p[0] += 0.8*(rng.choice([-1.0,1.0]))
       try:
          res=minimize(coarse_objective, p, args=(B,m), method='Nelder-Mead', options={'maxiter':1200,'xatol':1e-3,'fatol':1e-6})
          if res.success or res.fun < best_score:
              evaluate p=res.x
       except Exception:
          evaluate p
       update
       scale *=0.9
    return best_a
```

Softmax function:
```
def safe_softmax(z):
    z = z - np.max(z)
    e = np.exp(z)
    s = e.sum()
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0: return np.full(z.size, 1.0/z.size)
    e = np.maximum(e, 1e-12)
    e /= e.sum()
    return e
```
This re-clamps.

Helper details:
```
def as_valid_sequence(x, fallback_n=256):
    try arr=np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64)
    except: return None
    if arr.ndim!=1 or arr.size==0: return None
    arr=np.clip(arr,0,None)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(arr)) or arr.sum() < 1e-8: return None
    return arr/arr.sum()
```

`fft_sequence_score(a)`:
```
def fft_sequence_score(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,float)
    if a.size==0: return np.inf
    a=np.clip(a,0,None)
    s=a.sum()
    if s<1e-12: return np.inf
    c=fft_secure_convolution(a/s)
    if c.size==0: return np.inf
    val = float(2*c.size? no n=a.size * c.max()) because sum=1. Use 2*n*c.max().
    if not np.isfinite(val): return np.inf
    return val
```

Coarse objective:
```
def coarse_objective(params, basis, length):
    z = np.asarray(params,float)
    z = np.clip(z, -8, 8) # since exp
    val = np.tensordot(basis, z, axes=1)
    val = val - val.max()
    a = np.exp(val)
    a = np.where(a > 0, a, 1e-8)
    s = a.sum()
    if s <= 1e-12: return 1e6
    a /= s
    c = fft_secure_convolution(a)
    return float(2*length*c.max())
```
`np.tensordot` okay. Or `basis @ params`.

Coarse candidate:
```
def make_coarse_candidate(seed, budget_s, length):
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    deadline = time.monotonic() + max(0.0, budget_s)
    x = (np.arange(length)+0.5)/length
    h = 2*x - 1
    basis = np.empty((8, length))
    ...
    best = None; best_score = np.inf
    scale = 1.0
    max_starts = 16
    for s in range(max_starts):
        if time.monotonic() > deadline - 1: break
        p = rng.normal(0.0, scale, 8)
        # add one nonzero bias
        p[0] += 0.9 * (1.0 if rng.random()<0.5 else -1.0)
        p[3] += 0.5 * (1.0 if rng.random()<0.5 else -1.0)
        # evaluate p0
        scorep = coarse_objective(p,basis,length); update
        if _HAS_SCIPY:
            try:
                res = _scipy_minimize(coarse_objective, p, args=(basis,length), method='Nelder-Mead', options={'maxiter':800,'xatol':1e-3,'fatol':1e-7})
                # evaluate res.x
            except Exception: pass
        else:
            # simple coordinate random perturb 200
        scale *= 0.85
    if best is None: best=np.ones(length)/length
    return best,best_score
```
Need avoid evaluating too many if time. `coarse_objective` uses FFT length next pow2 ~2048, very fast. Nelder 800*16* maybe ok. But `maxiter` for Nelder can be low? Actually 2048 FFT each ~50us? 12k ~0.6s plus overhead maybe 5s. Good.

Sequence construction from coarse to n:
```
def upscale_sequence(a, target_n):
    a = np.asarray(a,float)
    m=a.size
    if m == target_n: return a.copy()
    if m < target_n:
       x_old=(np.arange(m)+0.5)/m
       x_new=(np.arange(target_n)+0.5)/target_n
       b=np.interp(x_new, x_old, a)
    else:
       # downsample by np.interp old x to new x? If m>target, not likely
       x_old=(np.arange(m)+0.5)/m
       x_new=(np.arange(target_n)+0.5)/target_n
       b=np.interp(x_new, x_old, a)
    b=np.clip(b,0,None); s=b.sum(); if s<=0: b=np.full(target_n,1/target_n)
    return b/s
```
If coarse m=1024 -> smooth.

def make_tilt_sequence(n, alpha):
   x=(np.arange(n)+0.5)/n
   h=2*x-1
   raw=np.exp(alpha*h)
   raw /= raw.sum(); return raw
```

Block helper:
```
def block_perturb(a, rng, strength):
   n=a.size
   width=int(rng.integers(max(1,n//2000), max(2,n//20)))
   start=int(rng.integers(0,n-width+1))
   cand=a.copy()
   cand[start:start+width] *= np.exp(rng.normal(0, strength, width))
   if rng.random()<0.2: cand=0.5*(cand+cand[::-1])? Avoid symmetry? Maybe don't sym.
   s=cand.sum(); if s>0: cand/=s
   return cand
def block_search(a, deadline, rng):

    try: arr=np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64)

    return arr

def _refine_sequence(a, deadline, rng):
    a = _valid_sequence(a)
    if a is None:
        n = int(getattr(a? no)); use n=256; return np.ones(n)/n, _fft_score(...)
    n=a.size
    if n == 0: ...
    best=a.copy(); best_score=_fft_score(a)
    # init z
    z = np.log(np.clip(a, 1e-8, None))
    z = z - z.mean()
    N=1; need=3*n-2; if need<1: need=1
    while N < need: N <<=1
    q_idx = (N - np.arange(n)) % N
    K = max(16, min(128, n//16?)) maybe n=4498 ->128? Use 64.
    m_v=np.zeros(n); v_v=np.zeros(n); t=0
    stagnant=0
    last_best_time? not needed.
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        t += 1
        a = _safe_softmax(z)
        if t % 1000 ==0:
           a = 0.999*a + 0.001*(1.0/n)
           a/=a.sum()
        fa=np.fft.rfft(a,N)
        c=np.fft.irfft(fa*fa,N)[:2*n-1].real
        if not np.isfinite(c).all(): break
        cmax = float(c.max())
        if cmax < best_score/(2*n) - 1e-12:
            best = a.copy(); best_score = 2*n*cmax; stagnant=0
        else:
            stagnant += 1
        # p
        if cmax <= 0: break
        if K < c.size:
            top = np.argpartition(c, -K)[-K:]
        else: top=np.arange(c.size)
        vals = c[top]
        temp = max(0.15*cmax, 1e-15)
        w = np.exp((vals - vals.max())/temp)
        wsum = w.sum()
        if wsum <=0 or not finite: w = np.ones_like(vals)/vals.size
        p = np.zeros(c.size, dtype=np.float64)
        p[top] = w/wsum
        # random break
        if rng.random() < 0.01:
            cand_scores = c > 0.85*cmax
            if cand_scores.any():
                top_alt = np.flatnonzero(cand_scores)
                # replace one random top index
                jp = int(rng.integers(0, len(top)-1))
                top[jj] = int(rng.choice(top_alt))
                vals=c[top]; recompute w? maybe if small. To avoid complexity, skip.
        fp=np.fft.rfft(p,N)
        cross=np.fft.irfft(np.conj(fp)*fa,N).real
        q = cross[q_idx]
        mgrad = float(np.dot(p,c))
        grad = 2.0*a*(q - mgrad)
        # tiny regularization to uniform
        grad = 0.999*grad + 0.001*(a - 1.0/n)
        # keep finite
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(grad)):
            grad = np.zeros(n)
        # Adam
        beta1=0.9; beta2=0.999; eps=1e-8
        m_v = beta1*m_v + (1-beta1)*grad
        v_v = beta2*v_v + (1-beta2)*grad*grad
        m_hat = m_v/(1-beta1**t)
        v_hat = v_v/(1-beta2**t)
        lr = 0.05 * (0.3 + 0.7*min(1.0, 300.0/max(1,t)))?)
```

```
def _make_coarse_candidate(seed, budget_s, target_n):
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    deadline=time.monotonic()+max(0.0,budget_s)
    length = max(256, min(target_n, 4096))
    x=(np.arange(length)+0.5)/length
    h=2*x-1
    basis=np.empty((8,length),float)
    basis[0]=h
    basis[1]=h*h-1/12
    basis[2]=h*h*h
    basis[3]=np.sin(np.pi*x)
    basis[4]=np.cos(np.pi*x)
    basis[5]=np.sin(2*np.pi*x)
    basis[6]=np.cos(2*np.pi*x)
    basis[7]=np.sin(3*np.pi*x)
    best=None; best_score=np.inf; scale=1.0
    max_starts = 10
    if time.monotonic() < deadline:
       for s in range(max_starts):
          if time.monotonic() > deadline - 1: break
          p0 = rng.normal(0, scale, 8)
          # biases
          p0[0] += 0.7*(1 if rng.random()<0.5 else -1)
          p0[4] += 0.7*(1 if rng.random()<0.5 else -1)
          # evaluate
          score0=_coarse_objective(p0,basis,length); p_good=p0; sc_good=score0
          # local Nelder
          if _HAS_SCIPY:
             try:
                res = _scipy_minimize(_coarse_objective, p0, args=(basis,length), method='Nelder-Mead', options={'maxiter':400,'xatol':1e-4,'fatol':1e-8})
                sc = float(res.fun)
                if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < sc_good:
                    p_good = np.asarray(res.x,float); sc_good=sc
             except Exception: pass
          else:
             # coordinate random search
             p_best=p_good.copy()
             for _ in range(200):
                if time.monotonic() > deadline-1: break
                p_try=p_best + rng.normal(0,0.2,8)
                sc_try=_coarse_objective(p_try,basis,length)
                if sc_try < sc_good: p_best=p_try; sc_good=sc_try
          if sc_good < best_score:
             best_score=sc_good
             try:
                a = _coarse_params_to_sequence(p_good,basis,length)
                if a is not None:
                    best=a
             except: pass
          scale *=0.8
    if best is None:
       best=np.ones(length)/length
    return best, best_score
```

def _coarse_params_to_sequence(params,basis,length):
    z=basis @ params
    z=np.clip(z,-10,10)
    a=np.exp(z-z.max())
    s=a.sum(); if s<=... return None
    return a/s
```

`_coarse_objective` can call `_coarse_params_to_sequence` but that does max; okay. But it needs score. Implement separately for speed:
```
def _coarse_objective(params,basis,length):
   try:
     z=np.asarray(params,float).clip(-8,8)
     val=basis.dot(z)
     val -= val.max()
     a=np.exp(val)
     s=a.sum()
     if s<=1e-12: return 1e6
     a/=s
     c=_fft_conv(a)
     return float(2*length*c.max())
   except: return 1e6
```
`params` from Nelder can be list; `np.asarray`.

Potential: Nelder-Mead may evaluate parameters where basis@params has large values; clip z -8/8 prevents exp overflow. Good.

`_upscale_sequence`:
```
def _upscale_sequence(a, target_n):
   a=_valid_sequence(a)
   if a is None: return np.full(max(1,target_n), 1.0/max(1,target_n))
   n=a.size
   if n == target_n: return a
   x_old=(np.arange(n)+0.5)/n
   x_new=(np.arange(target_n)+0.5)/target_n
   b=np.interp(x_new, x_old, a)
   b=np.clip(b,0,None); s=b.sum(); return b/s if s>0 else ones
```
If n>target, np.interp to fewer points ok.

Block search helper:
```
def _block_perturb(a, rng):
    n=a.size
    if n <= 2: return a.copy()
    lo = max(1, n//5000)
    hi = max(lo+1, n//20)
    w = int(rng.integers(lo, hi+1))
    start = int(rng.integers(0, n-w+1))
    cand = a.copy()
    cand[start:start+w] *= float(np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, 0.12, w)))
    if rng.random()<0.3:
       # small global log tilt

Block search:
```
def _block_search(a, deadline, rng):
    best=_valid_sequence(a); if None return None,inf
    best=best.copy(); best_score=_fft_score(best); strength=0.12; stagnant=0
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
       cand=_block_perturb(best,rng)
       sc=_fft_score(cand)
       if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
          best=cand; best_score=sc; stagnant=0
       else: stagnant+=1
       if stagnant > 1000:
          strength = max(0.03, strength*0.9)
          stagnant=0
    return best,best_score
```

```
S = [0]  # include 0? It creates sum0 count1; fine. Could start with 0 to minimize range. Or start with random? Greedy from 0 gives smallest possible set, likely max size for range.
counts = np.zeros(2*n-1, dtype=int32)
counts[0]=1
last=0
while len(S) < desired_m and last+1 < n and time:
    x = last+1
    while x < n:
       # check
       ok = counts[2*x] + 1 <= K
       if ok:
          s_sums = x + np.array(S)  # vector
          # Need counts[s_sums] all <= K-2; since new x not in S.
          if counts[s_sums].max() <= K-2:
             # add x
             counts[x + np.array(S)] += 2
             counts[2*x] += 1
             S.append(x); last=x; break
       x += 1
    else: break
```

Detailed greedy function:
```
def _make_sparse_candidate(n, K, desired_m, deadline, rng maybe):
    max_len=2*n-1
    counts=np.zeros(max_len, dtype=np.int16)
    S=[]
    # choose initial element 0
    S.append(0); counts[0]=1; last=0
    best_prefix=None; best_score=np.inf
    while len(S) < desired_m and last+1 < n and time...:
        found=False
        x=last+1
        # maybe consider up to last+some window? To avoid too slow, but all okay.
        while x < n:
            diag = counts[2*x] + 1
            if diag <= K:
                sums = x + np.asarray(S)  # each time S numpy? maintain Snp
                if counts[sums].max() <= K-2:
                    # add
                    counts[sums] += 2
                    counts[2*x] += 1
                    S.append(x); last=x; found=True; break
            x += 1
        if not found: break
        if len(S) >= 4:
            # build vector, score
            a=np.zeros(n); a[S]=1.0/len(S)
            sc=2*n*counts.max()/ (len(S)**2)  # counts.max() from current counts? counts max≤K exact. Use counts.max()
            if sc < best_score: best_prefix=S.copy(); best_score=sc
    # if best none, return first prefix
```

Implement a heuristic sparse builder:
For each new element, consider candidates in a sliding window of next W (e.g., 200 or 1000) integers after last. For each x, compute `penalty = max(counts[x+S_arr], counts[2*x]+1?)` or sum. Choose x with minimal penalty, perhaps with small random tie. Add it. This balances and may pack better. Complexity per element: W candidates * m vectors.     if n < 4: ...

    best_m=1; best_max=1

                # vector check
                # S_arr[:cnt] are elements
                sub=counts[x + S_arr[:cnt]]

                    counts[x + S_arr[:cnt]] += 2
                    counts[2*x] += 1
                    S_arr[cnt]=x; cnt+=1; last=x; found=True; break
            x += 1
        if not found: break
        current_max = int(counts.max())
        score = 2.0*n*current_max/(float(cnt)*float(cnt))
        if score < best_score: best...
    if best_m < 5: return None,inf
    a=np.zeros(n); a[S_arr[:best_m]]=1.0/best_m
    return a, best_score

In `_valid_sequence`, if seq is list, `np.asarray` may be empty. Return clipped normalized.

`_fft_convolution`:
```
def _fft_convolution(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)
    size = 2*n - 1
    N = 1
    while N < size:
        N <<= 1
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
    out = np.fft.irfft(fa*fa, N)[:size]
    return out
```

`_fft_score`:
```
def _fft_score(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    if a.size == 0: return np.inf
    a = np.clip(a,0,None)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 1e-12 or not np.isfinite(s): return np.inf
    c = _fft_convolution(a/s)
    if c.size == 0: return np.inf
    m = float(c.max())
    if not np.isfinite(m): return np.inf
    return float(2.0 * a.size * m)
```

`_append_candidate` as above.

`_make_sparse_candidate`:
```
def _make_sparse_candidate(n, max_pair_count, max_size, deadline):
    n=int(n); max_size=min(int(max_size), n)
    if n < 4 or max_size < 3: return None, np.inf
    max_len=2*n-1
    counts=np.zeros(max_len, dtype=np.int16)
    store=np.empty(n, dtype=np.int64)
    store[0]=0; cnt=1; last=0; counts[0]=1
    best_store=store[:1].copy(); best_m=1; best_score=float(2*n) # max1
    for _ in range(max_size):
        if time.monotonic() >= deadline: break
        found=False
        x=last+1
        while x < n:
            if counts[2*x] + 1 <= max_pair_count:
                sums = x + store[:cnt]
                # np.maximum? sub_max = counts[sums].max()
                if int(counts[sums].max()) <= max_pair_count - 2:
                    counts[sums] += 2
                    counts[2*x] += 1
                    store[cnt]=x; cnt += 1; last=x; found=True
                    break
            x += 1
        if not found: break
        cur_max = int(counts.max())
        sc = float(2.0*n*cur_max)/(float(cnt)*float(cnt))
        if sc < best_score:
            best_score=cur_max? no sc; best_store=store[:cnt].copy(); best_m=cnt
    if best_m < 4: return None, np.inf
    a=np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    a[best_store[:best_m]] = 1.0 / float(best_m)

```
def _coarse_params_sequence(params,basis):
   z=np.asarray(params,float).clip(-8,8)
   val=basis.dot(z)
   val=val-val.max()
   a=np.exp(val)
   s=a.sum()
   if s <= 1e-12 or not finite: return None
   return a/a.sum()
```

```
def _coarse_objective(params,basis,length):
   a=_coarse_params_sequence(params,basis)
   if a is None: return 1e6
   c=_fft_convolution(a)
   return float(2*length*c.max())
```

`_safe_softmax`:
```
def _safe_softmax(z):
   z=np.asarray(z,float)
   if not np.all(np.isfinite(z)): return np.full(z.size, 1/z.size)
   z = z - np.max(z)
   e = np.exp(z)
   e = np.maximum(e, 1e-12)
   s=e.sum()
   if not np.isfinite(s) or s<=0: return uniform
   return e/s
```

    short_each = max(8.0, (short_end - time.monotonic()) / max(1.0, len(top)))

       if ...

    # final exact

    return [float(v) for v in best]

```python
import numpy as np
import time

try:
    from scipy.optimize import minimize as _scipy_minimize
    _HAS_SCIPY = True
except Exception:
    _HAS_SCIPY = False


def _valid_sequence(seq):
    try:
        arr = np.asarray(seq, dtype=np.float64)
    except Exception:
        return None
    if arr.ndim != 1 or arr.size == 0:
        return None
    arr = np.clip(arr, 0.0, None)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(arr)):
        return None
    s = float(arr.sum())
    if s < 1e-8:
        return None
    return arr / s


def _fft_convolution(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)
    size = 2 * n - 1
    N = 1
    while N < size:
        N <<= 1
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
    return np.fft.irfft(fa * fa, N)[:size]


def _fft_score(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.inf
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, None)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s < 1e-12:
        return np.inf
    c = _fft_convolution(a / s)
    if c.size == 0:
        return np.inf
    m = float(c.max())
    if not np.isfinite(m):
        return np.inf
    return float(2.0 * a.size * m)


def _append_candidate(seq_list, score_list, seq):
    a = _valid_sequence(seq)
    if a is None:
        return
    s = _fft_score(a)
    if np.isfinite(s):
        seq_list.append(a)
        score_list.append(float(s))


def _make_sparse_candidate(n, max_pair_count, max_size, deadline):
    n = int(n)
    max_size = int(min(max_size, n))
    if n < 4 or max_size < 4:
        return None, np.inf
    counts = np.zeros(2 * n - 1, dtype=np.int16)
    store = np.empty(n, dtype=np.int64)
    store[0] = 0
    cnt = 1
    last = 0
    counts[0] = 1
    best_store = store[:1].copy()
    best_m = 1
    best_score = float(2.0 * n)

    for _ in range(max_size):
        if time.monotonic() >= deadline:
            break
        found = False
        x = last + 1
        while x < n:
            if counts[2 * x] + 1 <= max_pair_count:
                sums = x + store[:cnt]
                if int(counts[sums].max()) <= max_pair_count - 2:
                    counts[sums] += 2
                    counts[2 * x] += 1
                    store[cnt] = x
                    cnt += 1
                    last = x
                    found = True
                    break
            x += 1
        if not found:
            break
        cur_max = int(counts.max())
        score = float(2.0 * n * cur_max) / (float(cnt) * float(cnt))
        if score < best_score:
            best_score = score
            best_m = cnt
            best_store = store[:cnt].copy()

    if best_m < 4:
        return None, np.inf
    a = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    a[best_store[:best_m]] = 1.0 / float(best_m)
    return a, _fft_score(a)


def _coarse_params_sequence(params, basis):
    z = np.asarray(params, dtype=np.float64)
    z = np.clip(z, -8.0, 8.0)
    val = basis.dot(z)
    val = val - np.max(val)
    a = np.exp(val)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s < 1e-12:
        return None
    return a / s


def _coarse_objective(params, basis, length):
    a = _coarse_params_sequence(params, basis)
    if a is None:
        return 1e6
    c = _fft_convolution(a)
    m = float(c.max())
    if not np.isfinite(m):
        return 1e6
    return float(2.0 * length * m)


def _make_coarse_candidate(seed, budget_s, target_n):
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    deadline = time.monotonic() + max(0.0, float(budget_s))
    length = int(max(256, min(target_n, 4096)))
    x = (np.arange(length) + 0.5) / float(length)
    h = 2.0 * x - 1.0
    basis = np.empty((8, length), dtype=np.float64)
    basis[0] = h
    basis[1] = h * h - 1.0 / 12.0
    basis[2] = h * h * h
    basis[3] = np.sin(np.pi * x)
    basis[4] = np.cos(np.pi * x)
    basis[5] = np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * x)
    basis[6] = np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * x)
    basis[7] = np.sin(3.0 * np.pi * x)

    best_a = None
    best_score = np.inf
    scale = 1.0
    for _ in range(10):
        if time.monotonic() >= deadline - 1.0:
            break
        p0 = rng.normal(0.0, scale, 8)
        p0[0] += 0.7 * (1.0 if rng.random() < 0.5 else -1.0)
        p0[4] += 0.5 * (1.0 if rng.random() < 0.5 else -1.0)
        p_best = p0.copy()
        s_best = _coarse_objective(p_best, basis, length)

        if _HAS_SCIPY:
            try:
                res = _scipy_minimize(
                    _coarse_objective,
                    p_best,
                    args=(basis, length),
                    method="Nelder-Mead",
                    options={"maxiter": 350, "xatol": 1e-4, "fatol": 1e-8},
                )
                sx = float(res.fun)
                if np.isfinite(sx) and sx < s_best:
                    p_best = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64)
                    s_best = sx
            except Exception:
                pass
        else:
            for __ in range(120):
                if time.monotonic() >= deadline - 1.0:
                    break
                p_try = p_best + rng.normal(0.0, 0.2, 8)
                sx = _coarse_objective(p_try, basis, length)
                if np.isfinite(sx) and sx < s_best:
                    p_best = p_try
                    s_best = sx

        if np.isfinite(s_best) and s_best < best_score:
            best_score = float(s_best)
            a = _coarse_params_sequence(p_best, basis)
            if a is not None:
                best_a = a
        scale *= 0.85

    if best_a is None:
        best_a = np.ones(length, dtype=np.float64) / float(length)
    return best_a, float(best_score)


def _upscale_sequence(a, target_n):
    a = _valid_sequence(a)
    target_n = int(max(1, target_n))
    if a is None:
        return np.full(target_n, 1.0 / float(target_n), dtype=np.float64)
    m = a.size
    if m == target_n:
        return a.copy()
    x_old = (np.arange(m) + 0.5) / float(m)
    x_new = (np.arange(target_n) + 0.5) / float(target_n)
    b = np.interp(x_new, x_old, a)
    b = np.clip(b, 0.0, None)
    s = float(b.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s < 1e-12:
        return np.full(target_n, 1.0 / float(target_n), dtype=np.float64)
    return b / s


def _safe_softmax(z):
    z = np.asarray(z, dtype=np.float64)
    n = z.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(z)):
        return np.full(n, 1.0 / n, dtype=np.float64)
    z = z - np.max(z)
    e = np.exp(z)
    e = np.maximum(e, 1e-12)
    s = float(e.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s < 1e-12:
        return np.full(n, 1.0 / n, dtype=np.float64)
    return e / s


def _refine_sequence(a, deadline, rng):
    a = _valid_sequence(a)
    if a is None:
        return None, np.inf
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return None, np.inf

    best = a.copy()
    best_score = _fft_score(best)
    z = np.log(np.clip(a, 1e-8, None))
    z = z - np.mean(z)
    z_best = z.copy()

    need = max(1, 3 * n - 2)
    N = 1
    while N < need:
        N <<= 1
    q_idx = (N - np.arange(n)) % N
    k_lags = max(16, min(128, n // 32))

    m_v = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    v_v = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    om1 = 0.0
    om2 = 0.0
    beta1 = 0.9
    beta2 = 0.999
    t = 0
    stagnant = 0

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        t += 1
        if t > 200000:
            break
        a = _safe_softmax(z)
        if t % 2000 == 0:
            a = 0.999 * a + 0.001 * (1.0 / n)
            a /= a.sum()

        fa = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
        c = np.fft.irfft(fa * fa, N)[:2 * n - 1].real
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(c)):
            break
        cmax = float(max(0.0, c.max()))
        cur_score = float(2.0 * n * cmax)

        if np.isfinite(cur_score) and cur_score < best_score - 1e-12:
            best = a.copy()
            best_score = cur_score
            z_best = z.copy()
            stagnant = 0
        else:
            stagnant += 1

        if stagnant > 500 and cur_score > 2.0 * best_score + 0.5:
            z = z_best.copy()
            m_v.fill(0.0)
            v_v.fill(0.0)
            om1 = 0.0
            om2 = 0.0
            stagnant = 0
            continue

        if cmax <= 0.0:
            break

        if k_lags < c.size:
            top = np.argpartition(c, -k_lags)[-k_lags:]
        else:
            top = np.arange(c.size)
        vals = c[top]
        temp = max(0.25 * cmax, 1e-15)
        w = np.exp((vals - np.max(vals)) / temp)
        wsum = float(w.sum())
        if not np.isfinite(wsum) or wsum <= 0.0:
            w = np.ones_like(vals, dtype=np.float64)
            wsum = float(w.size)

        p = np.zeros(c.size, dtype=np.float64)
        p[top] = w / wsum

        fp = np.fft.rfft(p, N)
        cross = np.fft.irfft(np.conj(fp) * fa, N).real
        q = cross[q_idx]
        mgrad = float(np.dot(p, c))
        grad = 2.0 * a * (q - mgrad)
        grad = 0.999 * grad + 0.001 * (a - 1.0 / n)

        if not np.all(np.isfinite(grad)):
            grad = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

        om1 = 1.0 - beta1 + beta1 * om1
        om2 = 1.0 - beta2 + beta2 * om2
        m_v = beta1 * m_v + (1.0 - beta1) * grad
        v_v = beta2 * v_v + (1.0 - beta2) * grad * grad
        lr = 0.02 + 0.04 * (300.0 / (300.0 + float(t)))
        z = z - lr * (m_v / om1) / (np.sqrt(v_v / om2) + 1e-8)
        z = z - np.mean(z)

        if stagnant > 800:
            sigma = 0.08 if stagnant > 3000 else 0.03
            z = z + sigma * rng.normal(0.0, 1.0, n)
            z = z - np.mean(z)
            stagnant = 0

    final_score = _fft_score(best)
    if not np.isfinite(final_score):
        final_score = best_score
    return best, float(final_score)


def _block_perturb(a, rng, strength):
    n = a.size
    if n <= 2:
        return a.copy()
    lo = max(1, n // 5000)
    hi = max(lo + 1, n // 16)
    w = int(rng.integers(lo, hi + 1))
    start = int(rng.integers(0, n - w + 1))
    b = a.copy()
    b[start:start + w] *= float(np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, strength, w), dtype=np.float64))
    s = float(b.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return a.copy()
    return b / s


def _block_search(a, deadline, rng):
    a = _valid_sequence(a)
    if a is None:
        return None, np.inf
    best = a.copy()
    best_score = _fft_score(best)
    strength = 0.12
    stagnant = 0
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        cand = _block_perturb(best, rng, strength)
        sc = _fft_score(cand)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score - 1e-12:
            best = cand
            best_score = float(sc)
            stagnant = 0
        else:
            stagnant += 1
        if stagnant > 2000:
            strength = max(0.03, strength * 0.9)
            stagnant = 0
    return best, best_score


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1100, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    total_budget = max(1.0, float(budget_s))
    deadline = t0 + max(1.0, total_budget - 10.0)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)

    parent = None
    try:
        parent = _valid_sequence(height_sequence_1)
    except Exception:
        parent = None

    if parent is None:
        n = 1024
        parent = np.full(n, 1.0 / float(n), dtype=np.float64)
    else:
        n = int(parent.size)
        if n < 1:
            n = 1024
            parent = np.full(n, 1.0 / float(n), dtype=np.float64)

    seqs = []
    scores = []

    _append_candidate(seqs, scores, parent)

    sparse_deadline = min(deadline, t0 + max(5.0, min(20.0, 0.02 * total_budget)))
    max_m = int(min(n, max(80, 5.0 * np.sqrt(n) + 80.0)))
    for kpc in (2, 3, 4, 6, 8, 12, 16):
        if time.monotonic() >= sparse_deadline:
            break
        a, _ = _make_sparse_candidate(n, kpc, max_m, sparse_deadline)
        _append_candidate(seqs, scores, a)

    x = (np.arange(n) + 0.5) / float(n)
    h = 2.0 * x - 1.0
    for alpha in (-0.6, 0.6, -1.2, 1.2):
        raw = np.exp(alpha * h)
        raw /= raw.sum()
        _append_candidate(seqs, scores, raw)

    _append_candidate(seqs, scores, np.full(n, 1.0 / float(n), dtype=np.float64))

    if not scores:
        best = parent.copy()
        best_score = np.inf
    else:
        order = np.argsort(scores)
        best = seqs[int(order[0])].copy()
        best_score = float(scores[int(order[0])])

    coarse_budget = min(90.0, max(0.0, (deadline - time.monotonic()) * 0.25))
    if np.isfinite(best_score) and best_score > 1.8 and coarse_budget > 25.0:
        a, sc = _make_coarse_candidate(seed, coarse_budget, n)
        _append_candidate(seqs, scores, a)
        if len(scores) > 1:
            order = np.argsort(scores)
            if scores[int(order[0])] < best_score:
                best = seqs[int(order[0])].copy()
                best_score = float(scores[int(order[0])])

    if scores:
        order = np.argsort(scores)
        top = [seqs[int(i)].copy() for i in order[:6]]
    else:
        top = [best.copy()]

    short_end = min(deadline, t0 + max(60.0, min(240.0, 0.18 * total_budget)))
    remaining_candidates = max(1, len(top))
    short_each = max(8.0, min(35.0, float(short_end - time.monotonic()) / remaining_candidates))
    for a in top[:5]:
        if time.monotonic() >= short_end:
            break
        dl = min(short_end, time.monotonic() + max(5.0, short_each))
        b, sc = _refine_sequence(a, dl, rng)
        if b is not None and np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
            best = b
            best_score = sc

    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 30.0:
        b, sc = _refine_sequence(best, deadline - 30.0, rng)
        if b is not None and np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
            best = b
            best_score = sc

    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 5.0:
        b, sc = _block_search(best, deadline - 5.0, rng)
        if b is not None and np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
            best = b
            best_score = sc

    if best is None or not np.all(np.isfinite(best)):
        best = np.full(n, 1.0 / float(n), dtype=np.float64)

    try:
        exact = evaluate_sequence(best.tolist())
    except Exception:
        exact = np.inf
    if not np.isfinite(exact):
        best = np.full(n, 1.0 / float(n), dtype=np.float64)
        try:
            exact = evaluate_sequence(best.tolist())
        except Exception:
            exact = np.inf

    return [float(v) for v in best]
```
